. It follows that,
\lim_{n\to\infty}\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}w_{n}/n=s
,
\lim_{n\to\infty}(k_{n}-\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}})w_{n}/n=1-s
,
\lim_{n\to\infty}\ell_{n}w_{n}/n=t
, and
\lim_{n\to\infty}(k_{n}-\ell_{n})w_{n}/n=1-t
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=2}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}(V_{nj,t}V_{nj,s})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=2}^{\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}\mathbb{E}(V_{nj,t}V_{nj,s})+\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}+1}^{\ell_{n}}\mathbb{E}(V_{nj,t}V_{nj,s})+\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}+1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}(V_{nj,t}V_{nj,s})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=2}^{\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)g(\lfloor ns\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{2}\lfloor ns\rfloor^{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}+1}^{\ell_{n}}\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}\frac{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)g(\lfloor ns\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{2}\lfloor ns\rfloor(n-\lfloor ns\rfloor)}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}+1}^{\ell_{n}}(j-1-\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}})\frac{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)g(\lfloor ns\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{2}(n-\lfloor ns\rfloor)^{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}+1}^{k_{n}}\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}\frac{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)g(\lfloor ns\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{\lfloor nt\rfloor\lfloor ns\rfloor(n-\lfloor nt\rfloor)(n-\lfloor ns\rfloor)}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}+1}^{k_{n}}(\ell_{n}-\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}})\frac{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)g(\lfloor ns\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{\lfloor nt\rfloor(n-\lfloor nt\rfloor)(n-\lfloor ns\rfloor)^{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}+1}^{k_{n}}(j-1-\ell_{n})\frac{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)g(\lfloor ns\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{(n-\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{2}(n-\lfloor ns\rfloor)^{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\{\frac{\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}(\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}-1)}{2}\cdot\frac{(n-\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{2}(n-\lfloor ns\rfloor)^{2}(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n^{6}}

\displaystyle-(\ell_{n}-\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}})\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}\cdot\frac{(n-\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{2}\lfloor ns\rfloor(n-\lfloor ns\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n^{6}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(\ell_{n}-\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}})(\ell_{n}-\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}-1)}{2}\cdot\frac{(n-\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{2}\lfloor ns\rfloor^{2}(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n^{6}}

\displaystyle+(k_{n}-\ell_{n})\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}\cdot\frac{\lfloor nt\rfloor\lfloor ns\rfloor(n-\lfloor nt\rfloor)(n-\lfloor ns\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n^{6}}

\displaystyle-(k_{n}-\ell_{n})(\ell_{n}-\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}})\cdot\frac{\lfloor nt\rfloor(n-\lfloor nt\rfloor)\lfloor ns\rfloor^{2}(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n^{6}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(k_{n}-\ell_{n})(k_{n}-\ell_{n}-1)}{2}\cdot\frac{\lfloor nt\rfloor^{2}\lfloor ns\rfloor^{2}(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n^{6}}\bigg\}\frac{\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})}{p}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle\frac{(1-t)^{2}s^{2}}{2}\lim_{p\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})}{p}.

If at least one point of
\lfloor ns\rfloor
and
\lfloor nt\rfloor
falls within a large block, the discussion is similar to the above.
Summarizing the above results, for
s<t
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{-2}\sum\limits_{j=2}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}(\tilde{V}_{nj,ts}^{2})=a^{2}\frac{t^{2}(1-t)^{2}}{4}+b^{2}\frac{s^{2}(1-s)^{2}}{4}+2ab\frac{(1-t)^{2}s^{2}}{4}.

Repeating the discussion in Proposition
8.3
, the Lindeberg condition can be easily obtained. Now, we have

\left(W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{(G)}-\mu_{t}^{(G)},W(\lfloor ns\rfloor)^{(G)}-\mu_{s}^{(G)}\right)^{\top}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\rightarrow}}N_{2}(\mathbf{0},\Xi),

where

\displaystyle\Xi=\omega^{2}\begin{pmatrix}(1-t)^{2}t^{2}&(1-t)^{2}s^{2}\\
(1-t)^{2}s^{2}&(1-s)^{2}s^{2}\end{pmatrix}.

More than three points can be treated in the same way and therefore the finite-dimensional distributions of
W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{(G)}-\mu_{t}^{(G)}
has been established.

Step 3.
Prove the tightness.
We also consider the following two cases, which has been discussed in Step 1.

(I)

there exists
\ell_{n}
such that
\ell_{n}
th block fall in the left side of the point
\lfloor nt\rfloor
and
(\ell_{n}+1)
th block fall in the right side of the point
\lfloor nt\rfloor
, that is
b_{\ell_{n}}^{r}\leq\lfloor nt\rfloor<b_{\ell_{n}+1}^{l}
;

(II)

there exists
\ell_{n}
such that the point
\lfloor nt\rfloor
fall in the
\ell_{n}
th block, that is
b_{\ell_{n}}^{l}\leq\lfloor nt\rfloor<b_{\ell_{n}}^{r}
.

Here we discuss only case (I), the discussion of case (II) proceeds analogously.
In case (II), we define

\displaystyle\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{i}=\frac{1}{w_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell},

and rewrite

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}\sum\limits_{j=2}^{k_{n}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t},

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-t)^{2}A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}-t(1-t)A_{\ell_{n}+1,k_{n}}^{1,\ell_{n}}+t^{2}A_{\ell_{n}+1,k_{n}}^{\ell_{n}+1,j-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-t)A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}-t(1-t)A_{2,k_{n}}^{1,j-1}+tA_{\ell_{n}+1,k_{n}}^{\ell_{n}+1,j-1},

where

\displaystyle A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}:=

\displaystyle\frac{2(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n\sqrt{p}}\sum\limits_{j=2}^{\ell_{n}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{i}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{j},

\displaystyle A_{\ell_{n}+1,k_{n}}^{1,\ell_{n}}:=

\displaystyle\frac{2(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n\sqrt{p}}\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}+1}^{k_{n}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\ell_{n}}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{i}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{j},

\displaystyle A_{\ell_{n}+1,k_{n}}^{\ell_{n}+1,j-1}:=

\displaystyle\frac{2(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n\sqrt{p}}\sum\limits_{j=\ell_{n}+1}^{k_{n}}\sum\limits_{i=\ell_{n}+1}^{j-1}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{i}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{j},

We now prove the tightness of
(1-t)\omega^{-1}A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1},t(1-t)\omega^{-1}A_{2,k_{n}}^{1,j-1},t\omega^{-1}A_{\ell_{n}+1,k_{n}}^{\ell_{n}+1,j-1}
.
In step 1, we have proved
\mathbb{E}\{(\omega^{-1}A_{2,k_{n}}^{1,j-1})^{2}\}\leq C
, where
C
is a constant. Then,
t(1-t)A_{2,k_{n}}^{1,j-1}
is tight.
Now, we resort to the tightness of
(1-t)A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}
. It suffices to prove that for any
\varsigma,\varphi>0
, there exists an integer
n_{0}\geq 1
and
\iota\in(0,1)
, such that

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\Big(\mathop{\mathrm{sup}}\limits_{s\leq t\leq s+\iota}\Big|(1-t)A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}-(1-s)A_{2,\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{1,j-1}\Big|\geq\omega\varsigma\Big)\leq\iota\varphi,\quad n\geq n_{0},

(14)

for all
s\in[0,1]
, where
\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}
satisfy
\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}w_{n}+1\leq\lfloor ns\rfloor\leq\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}w_{n}+w_{n}-M
.
Since

\displaystyle\Big|(1-t)A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}-(1-s)A_{2,\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{1,j-1}\Big|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|-(t-s)A_{2,\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{1,j-1}+(1-t)(A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}-A_{2,\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{1,j-1})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(t-s)\Big|A_{2,\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{1,j-1}\Big|+\Big|A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}-A_{2,\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{1,j-1}\Big|,

then inequality (
14
) follows if the following inequalities hold for all
s\in[0,1]
:

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\Big(\mathop{\mathrm{sup}}\limits_{s\leq t\leq s+\iota}(t-s)|A_{2,\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{1,j-1}|\geq\omega\frac{\varsigma}{2}\Big)\leq\frac{\iota\varphi}{2},\quad n\geq n_{0},

(15)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\Big(\mathop{\mathrm{sup}}\limits_{s\leq t\leq s+\iota}|A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}-A_{2,\ell_{n}^{{}^{\prime}}}^{1,j-1}|\geq\omega\frac{\varsigma}{2}\Big)\leq\frac{\iota\varphi}{2},\quad n\geq n_{0}.

(16)

To be noticed that, inequality (
15
) can be obtained from (
16
), and by Theorem 8.4 in
Billingsley 2013
, inequality (
16
) reduces to the following statement: there exists a
\lambda_{0}>1
and an integer
n_{0}
such that for all
h_{n}
satisfy

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\Big(\mathop{\max}\limits_{m_{n}\leq k_{n}}|A_{2,h_{n}+m_{n}}^{1,j-1}-A_{2,h_{n}}^{1,j-1}|\geq\omega\lambda_{0}\Big)\leq\frac{\varsigma}{\lambda_{0}^{2}},

for all
k_{n}\geq n_{0}
.
Since
\{\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\xi}}}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{k_{n}}
are i.i.d., the above inequality can reduced to

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\Big(\mathop{\max}\limits_{m_{n}\leq k_{n}-1}|A_{2,m_{n}}^{1,j-1}|\geq\omega\lambda_{0}\Big)\leq\frac{\varsigma}{\lambda_{0}^{2}},\quad n\geq n_{0}.

Since
\{A_{2,m_{n}}^{1,j-1};2\leq m_{n}\leq k_{n}\}
is a martingale with respect to
\sigma
-algebra
\{\mathcal{F}_{nm_{n}},m=2,\dots,k_{n}\}
. According to the Doob’s martingale inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\Big(\mathop{\max}\limits_{m_{n}\leq k_{n}}|A_{2,m_{n}}^{1,j-1}|\geq\omega\lambda_{0}\Big)\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}(|A_{1,m_{n}}^{1,j-1}|^{4})}{\omega^{4}\lambda_{0}^{4}}\leq\frac{\varsigma}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}

for sufficiently large
\lambda_{0}
because
\omega^{-4}\mathbb{E}(|A_{2,m_{n}}^{1,j-1}|^{4})\leq C
. Thus,
(1-t)\omega^{-1}A_{2,\ell_{n}}^{1,j-1}
is tight. The prove technique of tightness of
t\omega^{-1}A_{\ell_{n}+1,k_{n}}^{\ell_{n}+1,j-1}
is similar, the details are omitted here.
Therefore, the prove of Lemma
8.1
is complete by the above three main steps.

L
EMMA
8.2

Under Assumptions
1
–
2
and
H_{0}
, for any
t\in[0,1]
, we have

\omega^{-1}\{W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{(NG)}-W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{(G)}\}=o_{p}(1).

Proof.

For ease of presentation, we remark
B_{ij,t},D_{ij,t},F_{t}
in the proof of Lemma
8.1
as
B^{(G)}_{ij,t},D^{(G)}_{ij,t},F_{t}^{(G)}
. For non-Gaussian sequence
\{\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
, we can define
B^{(NG)}_{ij,t},D^{(NG)}_{ij,t},F_{t}^{(NG)}
, similarly.
Let
\Delta\mathcal{H}_{i,t}=\mathcal{H}_{i,t}^{(NG)}-\mathcal{H}_{i,t}^{(G)}
, then
W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{(NG)}-W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{(G)}=\sum\nolimits_{i=1}^{4}\Delta\mathcal{H}_{i,t}
.

Step 1.
In this step, we will show that
\omega^{-1}\Delta\mathcal{H}_{1,t}=o(1)
.
Define
{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}_{i,t}:=a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}
, and

\displaystyle\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t}:=\frac{1}{w_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}_{\ell,t},

we have

\displaystyle B_{ij,t}^{(G)}

\displaystyle=g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t},

\displaystyle B_{ij,t}^{(NG)}

\displaystyle=g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{j,t}.

Both
\{\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t}\}_{i=1}^{k_{n}}
and
\{\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t}\}_{i=1}^{k_{n}}
are independent sequence with zero mean and
\mathrm{var}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t})=\mathrm{var}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})=(w_{n}-M)^{-1}{\bf\Omega}_{w_{n}-M,M}
, where
{\bf\Omega}_{w_{n}-M,M}=\sum\limits_{h\in\mathcal{M}}\{1-(w_{n}-M)^{-1}|h|\}\Gamma_{M}(h)
.
Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},t}):=\frac{\mathcal{H}_{1,t}^{(G)}}{\sqrt{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}}=2\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\frac{B_{ij,t}^{(G)}}{\sqrt{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}}

\displaystyle=2\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t}),

where
u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t}):=g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)(w_{n}-M)^{2}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t}/\sqrt{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}
. Since
\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t}
and
\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t}
are linear combinations of
\boldsymbol{Z}_{i}=(Z_{i1},\dots,Z_{ip})^{\top}
and
\mathbb{E}(Z_{ij}^{4})<\infty
. We have the following properties:

(i)

\forall\boldsymbol{a}\in\mathcal{R}^{p}
,
\mathbb{E}\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t},\boldsymbol{a})\}=\mathbb{E}\{u(\boldsymbol{a},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t})\}=0
,
\mathbb{E}\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\boldsymbol{a})\}=\mathbb{E}\{u(\boldsymbol{a},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})\}=0
.

(ii)

\forall\boldsymbol{a},\boldsymbol{b}\in\mathcal{R}^{p}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{u(\boldsymbol{a},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t})u(\boldsymbol{b},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t})\}=\mathbb{E}\{u(\boldsymbol{a},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})u(\boldsymbol{b},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})\},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{u(\boldsymbol{a},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t})u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t},\boldsymbol{b})\}=\mathbb{E}\{u(\boldsymbol{a},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\boldsymbol{b})\},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t},\boldsymbol{a})u(\boldsymbol{b},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t})\}=\mathbb{E}\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\boldsymbol{a})u(\boldsymbol{b},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})\}.

(iii)

Let
g_{ij,t}^{2}:=\mathbb{E}\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{j,t})^{2}\}
, then

\displaystyle\max\left[\mathbb{E}\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{j,t})^{4}\},\mathbb{E}\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t})^{4}\},\mathbb{E}\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t})^{4}\}\right]\leq\rho_{0}g_{ij,t}^{4}<\infty,

where
\rho_{0}=\max(\tau_{1},3)
.

It is known that a sequence of random variables
\{Z_{n};n=1,\dots.\infty\}
converges weakly to a random variable
Z
if and only if for every
f\in\mathcal{C}_{b}^{3}(\mathbb{R})
,
\mathbb{E}\{f(Z_{n})\}\rightarrow\mathbb{E}\{f(Z)\}
, see,
Pollard 1984
. It reduces to prove

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\}-\mathbb{E}\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\}\right|\rightarrow 0.

for every
f\in\mathcal{C}_{b}^{3}(\mathbb{R})
as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
. For
i=1,\dots,k_{n}
, we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{i}

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i-1,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{i,0}

\displaystyle:=\sum\limits_{1\leq\ell_{1}<\ell_{2}\leq i-1}u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{\ell_{1},t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{\ell_{2},t})+\sum\limits_{i+1\leq\ell_{1}<\ell_{2}\leq k_{n}}u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{\ell_{1},t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{\ell_{2},t})+\sum\limits_{1\leq\ell_{1}<\leq i-1,i+1\leq\ell_{2}\leq k_{n}}u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{\ell_{1},t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}_{2},t}).

Thus,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\}-\mathbb{E}\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\}\right|\leq\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}\left|\mathbb{E}\{f(\mathcal{W}_{i})\}-\mathbb{E}\{f(\mathcal{W}_{i+1})\}\right|.

According to Taylor’s expansion, we have

\displaystyle f(\mathcal{W}_{i})-f(\mathcal{W}_{i,0})

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{m=1}^{2}\frac{1}{m!}f^{(m)}(\mathcal{W}_{i,0})(\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0})^{m}+O(|\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{3}),

\displaystyle f(\mathcal{W}_{i+1})-f(\mathcal{W}_{i,0})

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{m=1}^{2}\frac{1}{m!}f^{(m)}(\mathcal{W}_{i,0})(\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0})^{m}+O(|\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{3}).

Based on the properties of
u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i-1,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i+1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{m_{1}=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{m_{1},t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})|\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{m_{1},t}\right\}+\sum\limits_{m_{2}=i+1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{{m_{2}},t})|\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{m_{2},t}\right\}=0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i-1,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i+1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{m_{1}=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{m_{1},t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t})|\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{m_{1},t}\right\}+\sum\limits_{m_{2}=i+1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{{m_{2}},t})|\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{m_{2},t}\right\}=0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{(\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0})^{2}|\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i-1,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i+1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{(\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0})^{2}|\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i-1,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i+1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}\right\}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum\limits_{m=1}^{2}\frac{1}{m!}f^{(m)}(\mathcal{W}_{i,0})(\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0})^{m}\bigg\}=\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum\limits_{m=1}^{2}\frac{1}{m!}f^{(m)}(\mathcal{W}_{i,0})(\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0})^{m}\bigg\}.

It must exists a positive constant
C
such that

\displaystyle~~~~\left|\mathbb{E}\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\}-\mathbb{E}\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}\left(\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{3}+\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}\left\{(\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{4})^{3/4}+(\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{4})^{3/4}\right\}.

Now, we consider
\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{4}
and
\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{4}
. The discussions of these two terms are similar.
We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{W}_{i}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{\ell,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})^{4}\right\}+\sum\limits_{\ell=i+1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{\ell,t})^{4}\right\}+6\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=1}^{i-1}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=2}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{\ell_{1},t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})^{2}u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{\ell_{2},t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+6\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=i+1}^{k_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=i+1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{\ell_{1},t})^{2}u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{\ell_{2},t})^{2}\right\}+6\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=1}^{i-1}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=i+1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left\{u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{\ell_{1},t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t})^{2}u(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,t},\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{\ell_{2},t})^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho_{0}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{i-1}g_{\ell i,t}^{4}+\sum\limits_{\ell=i+1}^{k_{n}}g_{i\ell,t}^{4}+6\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=1}^{i-1}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=2}^{i-1}g_{\ell_{1}i,t}^{2}g_{\ell_{2}i,t}^{2}+6\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=i+1}^{k_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=i+1}^{k_{n}}g_{i\ell_{1},t}^{2}g_{i\ell_{2},t}^{2}

\displaystyle+6\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=1}^{i-1}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=i+1}^{k_{n}}g_{\ell_{1}i,t}^{2}g_{i\ell_{2},t}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\rho_{0}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{i-1}g_{\ell i,t}^{2}+\sum\limits_{\ell=i+1}^{k_{n}}g_{i\ell,t}^{2}\bigg)^{2},

and similarly,

\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{W}_{i+1}-\mathcal{W}_{i,0}|^{4}\leq 3\rho_{0}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{i-1}g_{\ell i,t}^{2}+\sum\limits_{\ell=i+1}^{k_{n}}g_{i\ell,t}^{2}\bigg)^{2}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\right\}\right|

\displaystyle=O\bigg\{\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{i-1}g_{\ell i,t}^{2}+\sum\limits_{\ell=i+1}^{k_{n}}g_{i\ell,t}^{2}\bigg)^{3/2}\bigg\}.

For any
i\neq j
, we have

\displaystyle g_{ij,t}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\}^{2}(w_{n}-M)^{4}\mathbb{E}\left\{(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,t}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,t})^{2}\right\}}{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\}^{2}}{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{3}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{4}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}\mathbb{E}({\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{\ell_{1},t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{\ell_{2},t}{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{\ell_{3},t}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{\ell_{4},t})

\displaystyle=\frac{\{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\}^{2}}{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{3}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{4}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell_{1},\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{\ell_{2},\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{\ell_{3},\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{\ell_{4},\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}({\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{2}}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{3}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{4}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cn^{2}}{n^{4}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}\mathrm{tr}\bigg\{\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{M}(|\ell_{1}-\ell_{2}|)\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=O\bigg\{\frac{(w_{n}-M)^{2}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})}{n^{2}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}\{1+o(1)\}\bigg\}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\big\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\big\}-\mathbb{E}\big\{f(\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{1,t},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},t}))\big\}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\bigg\{k_{n}\bigg(k_{n}\frac{(w_{n}-M)^{2}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M})}{n^{2}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}\bigg)^{3/2}\bigg\}=o(1).

Step 2.
In this step, we will show that
\omega^{-1}\Delta\mathcal{H}_{2,t}=o_{p}(1)
, where

\displaystyle\Delta\mathcal{H}_{2,t}=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}B^{(NG)}_{ii,t}-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}B^{(G)}_{ii,t}.

In the step 1 of Lemma
8.1
, we have prove that
\omega^{-1}\sum\nolimits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}B_{ii,t}=o_{p}(1)
, thus, this statement is proved.

Step 3.
In this step, we will show that
\omega^{-1}\Delta\mathcal{H}_{3,t}=o_{p}(1)
.

\displaystyle\Delta\mathcal{H}_{3,t}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{1\leq i,j\leq k_{n}}D^{(NG)}_{ij,t}-\sum\limits_{1\leq i,j\leq k_{n}}D^{(G)}_{ij,t}.

Let

\displaystyle L_{ij,t}

\displaystyle:=\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=iw_{n}-M+1}^{iw_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}F_{ij,t},

\displaystyle C_{ij,t}

\displaystyle:=\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=iw_{n}-M+1}^{iw_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=jw_{n}-M+1}^{jw_{n}}F_{ij,t},

Then,
D_{ij,t}=2L_{ij,t}+C_{ij,t}
. Furthermore, define

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1,t}:=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}-2}\sum\limits_{j=i+2}^{k_{n}}L_{ij,t},\quad\mathcal{L}_{2,t}:=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}L_{ii,t},\quad\mathcal{L}_{3,t}=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}L_{ii+1,t},

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1,t}:=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}-1}\sum\limits_{j=i+1}^{k_{n}}C_{ij,t},\quad\mathcal{C}_{2,t}:=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}C_{ii,t}.

Then,
\mathcal{H}_{3,t}=\sum\nolimits_{1\leq i,j\leq k_{n}}D_{ij,t}=4\mathcal{L}_{1,t}+2\mathcal{L}_{2,t}+4\mathcal{L}_{3,t}+2\mathcal{C}_{1,t}+\mathcal{C}_{2,t}
. We will prove that
\mathrm{var}(\omega^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{i,t})=o(1)
for
i=1,2,3
, and
\mathrm{var}(\omega^{-1}\mathcal{C}_{i,t})=o(1)
for
i=1,2
. Recall

\displaystyle F_{ij,t}^{(NG)}:=g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{j,\lfloor nt\rfloor}\left[\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}-\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{M}(|j-i|)\}\right].

We first consider
\mathrm{var}(\omega^{-1}\mathcal{L}^{(NG)}_{1,t})
.
Since
j_{1}\geq i_{1}+2
, which implies
\ell_{2}-\ell_{1}\geq M
,

\mathbb{E}\left\{F^{(NG)}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2},t}F^{(NG)}_{\ell_{3}\ell_{4},t}\right\}=\{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\}^{2}\prod\limits_{\nu=1}^{4}a_{\ell_{\nu},\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}(\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{2}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{3}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{4}})

is nonzero only if
i_{2}=i_{1}
or
i_{1}=j_{2}
or
i_{1}=j_{2}-1
.
If
i_{2}=i_{1}
,
\mathbb{E}(\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{2}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{3}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{4}})\neq 0
only if
j_{2}=j_{1}
. If
i_{1}=j_{2}
or
i_{1}=j_{2}-1
, then
i_{2}\leq j_{2}-2\leq i_{1}-1
, which implies
\ell_{(1)}-\ell_{(2)}=\mathrm{min}\{\ell_{1}-\ell_{3},\ell_{4}-\ell_{3}\}>M
. Therefore,
\mathbb{E}(\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{2}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{3}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{\ell_{4}})\neq 0
only if
i_{2}=i_{1}
and
j_{2}=j_{1}
. Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\mathcal{L}_{1,t}^{(NG)}\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{n}-2}\sum\limits_{j_{1}=i_{1}+2}^{k_{n}}\sum\limits_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{n}-2}\sum\limits_{j_{2}=i_{2}+2}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left(L^{NG}_{i_{1}j_{1},t}L^{NG}_{i_{2}j_{2},t}\right)

\displaystyle=\{g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\}^{2}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}-2}\sum\limits_{j=i+2}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=iw_{n}-M+1}^{iw_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell_{1},\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{\ell_{2},\lfloor nt\rfloor}\epsilon_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\epsilon_{\ell_{2}}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=O\bigg\{\frac{(k_{n}-1)(k_{n}-2)}{n^{2}p}(w_{n}-M)M\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n,M}{\bf\Omega}_{n,w_{n}-M})\bigg\}

\displaystyle=O\bigg\{\frac{(k_{n}-1)(k_{n}-2)(w_{n}-M)M}{n^{2}p}\cdot\{\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{n,M})\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{n,w_{n}-M})\}^{1/2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=o\left\{p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{n})\right\}.

Then
\omega^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{1,t}^{(NG)}=o_{p}(1)
. Similarly, we have
\omega^{-1}\mathcal{C}_{1,1t}^{(NG)}=o_{p}(1)
.
Next, we consider

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{(\mathcal{L}_{2,t}^{(NG)})^{2}\}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{n}}\sum\limits_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left(L^{(NG)}_{i_{1}i_{1},t}L^{(NG)}_{i_{2}i_{2},t}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{n}}\sum\limits_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=i_{1}w_{n}-M+1}^{i_{1}w_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(i_{1}-1)w_{n}+1}^{i_{1}w_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{3}=i_{2}w_{n}-M+1}^{i_{2}w_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{4}=(i_{2}-1)w_{n}+1}^{i_{2}w_{n}-M}F_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2},t}F_{\ell_{3}\ell_{4},t}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=iw_{n}-M+1}^{iw_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}F_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2},t}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=O\bigg\{\frac{k_{n}M^{2}(w_{n}-M)^{2}}{n^{2}p}\tau_{2}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n,M}^{2})\bigg\}=o\left\{p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{n})\right\},

where the forth equation holds based on Lemma
8.12
. Then
\omega^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{2,t}^{(NG)}=o_{p}(1)
. Similarly, we have
\omega^{-1}\mathcal{L}_{3,t}^{(NG)}=o_{p}(1)
,
\omega^{-1}\mathcal{C}_{2,t}^{(NG)}=o_{p}(1)
.

Step 4.
In this step, we will show that
\omega^{-1}\Delta\mathcal{H}_{4,t}=o_{p}(1)
.
Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}

\displaystyle:=\{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\{k_{n}w_{n}+1,\dots,n\}\times\{1,\dots,n\}\times\{k_{n}w_{n}+1,\dots,n\}\times\{1,\dots,n\}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\{1,\dots,n\}^{4}:|\ell_{1}-\ell_{3}|\geq 3M,\mathrm{min}(\ell_{1},\ell_{3})>k_{n}w_{n}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\{1,\dots,n\}^{4}:|\ell_{1}-\ell_{3}|\geq 3M,\mathrm{min}(\ell_{1},\ell_{3})>k_{n}w_{n},

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\mathrm{min}(|\ell_{1}-\ell_{2}|,|\ell_{1}-\ell_{4}|,|\ell_{3}-\ell_{2}|,|\ell_{3}-\ell_{4}|)\leq M\}.

For all
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{L}_{1}\backslash\mathcal{L}_{2}
, we have
\mathbb{E}(F_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2},t}F_{\ell_{3}\ell_{4},t})=0
. Since
\mathcal{L}_{2}\subset\{k_{n}w_{n}+1,\dots,n\}^{4}
, we have
|\mathcal{L}_{2}|\leq(w_{n}+M)^{4}
and
|\mathcal{L}\backslash\mathcal{L}_{1}|\leq 6Mnw_{n}^{2}
. Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(F_{t}^{(NG)}\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\bigg(2\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=1}^{k_{n}w_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=k_{n}w_{n}+1}^{n}F^{(NG)}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2},t}+\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=k_{n}w_{n}+1}^{n}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=k_{n}w_{n}+1}^{n}F^{(NG)}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2},t}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=k_{n}w_{n}+1}^{n}\sum\limits_{\ell_{3}=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{\ell_{4}=k_{n}w_{n}+1}^{n}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(F^{(NG)}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2},t}F^{(NG)}_{\ell_{3}\ell_{4},t}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=4\bigg(\sum\limits_{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{L}\backslash\mathcal{L}_{1}}+\sum\limits_{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{L}_{1}\backslash\mathcal{L}_{2}}+\sum\limits_{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{L}_{2}}\bigg)\left|\mathbb{E}\left(F^{(NG)}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2},t}F^{(NG)}_{\ell_{3}\ell_{4},t}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=O\bigg\{\frac{6Mnw_{n}^{2}+(w_{n}+M)^{4}}{n^{2}p}\tau_{2}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n,M}^{2})\bigg\}=o\left\{p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{n})\right\},

with Lemma
8.12
.

L
EMMA
8.3

Under Assumptions
1
–
2
and
H_{0}
, for any
t\in[0,1]
, we have

\omega^{-1}\{W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{(NG)}\}=o_{p}(1).

Proof.

According to the definitions, we have

\displaystyle W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)-W(\lfloor nt\rfloor)^{(NG)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{j,\lfloor nt\rfloor}(\boldsymbol{X}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{j,\lfloor nt\rfloor}(\boldsymbol{X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i})^{\top}(\boldsymbol{X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j})

\displaystyle+2g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{j,\lfloor nt\rfloor}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}).

Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}:=\{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4});\,i_{1}-M\leq\ell_{1}\leq i_{1},\ell_{2}\leq j_{1}-M-1,

\displaystyle i_{2}-M\leq\ell_{3}\leq i_{2},\ell_{4}\leq j_{2}-M-1\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{1}:=\{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4});\,\ell_{1}=\ell_{2}\neq\ell_{3}=\ell_{4}\},\quad\mathcal{B}_{2}:=\{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4});\,\ell_{1}=\ell_{3}\neq\ell_{2}=\ell_{4}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{3}:=\{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4});\,\ell_{1}=\ell_{4}\neq\ell_{2}=\ell_{3}\},\quad\mathcal{B}_{4}:=\{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4});\,\ell_{1}=\ell_{4}=\ell_{2}=\ell_{3}\}.

Considering

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\bigg\{\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j})\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq n\\
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{1}-\ell_{2}}b_{i_{2}-\ell_{3}}b_{j_{2}-\ell_{4}}\mathbb{E}\big[\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{2}}-\mathbb{I}(\ell_{1}=\ell_{2})\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\}\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{3}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{4}}-\mathbb{I}(\ell_{3}=\ell_{4})\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\}\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq n\\
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}_{1}\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{i_{2}-\ell_{3}}b_{j_{2}-\ell_{3}}\mathbb{E}\big[\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{1}}-\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\}\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{3}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{3}}-\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\}\big]

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq n\\
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}_{2}\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{1}-\ell_{2}}b_{i_{2}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{2}-\ell_{2}}\mathbb{E}\big\{(\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{2}})^{2}\}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq n\\
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}_{3}\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{1}-\ell_{2}}b_{i_{2}-\ell_{2}}b_{j_{2}-\ell_{1}}\mathbb{E}\big\{(\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{2}})(\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{2}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{1}})\big\}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq n\\
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}_{4}\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{i_{2}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{2}-\ell_{1}}\mathbb{E}\big[\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{\ell_{1}}-\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq n\\
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}_{2}\end{subarray}}\lvert b_{i_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{1}-\ell_{2}}b_{i_{2}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{2}-\ell_{2}}\rvert\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq n\\
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}_{3}\end{subarray}}\lvert b_{i_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{1}-\ell_{2}}b_{i_{2}-\ell_{2}}b_{j_{2}-\ell_{1}}\rvert\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2}\leq n\\
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3},\ell_{4})\in\mathcal{B}_{i_{1}j_{1}i_{2}j_{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}_{4}\end{subarray}}\lvert b_{i_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{1}-\ell_{1}}b_{i_{2}-\ell_{1}}b_{j_{2}-\ell_{1}}\rvert\tau_{1}\mathrm{tr}^{2}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{2}\bigg\{\bigg(\sum\limits_{i_{1},i_{2}\geq 0}|b_{i_{1}}b_{i_{2}}|\bigg)\bigg(\sum\limits_{j_{1},j_{2}\geq M}|b_{j_{1}}b_{j_{2}}|\bigg)+\bigg(\sum\limits_{i_{1}\geq 0,j_{2}\geq M}|b_{i_{1}}b_{j_{2}}|\bigg)\bigg(\sum\limits_{i_{2}\geq 0,j_{1}\geq M}|b_{i_{2}}b_{j_{1}}|\bigg)\bigg\}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\tau_{1}np\sum\limits_{i_{1},i_{2}\geq 0,j_{1},j_{2}\geq M}\lvert b_{i_{1}}b_{i_{2}}b_{j_{1}}b_{j_{2}}\rvert\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2}).

By Lemma
8.12
and Lemma
8.13
, we have

g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{j,\lfloor nt\rfloor}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j})=o\{p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{n})\}.

Similarly, we can prove

g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{j,\lfloor nt\rfloor}(\boldsymbol{X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i})^{\top}(\boldsymbol{X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j})=o\{p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{n})\}.

This completes Lemma
8.3
.

L
EMMA
8.4

Under Assumptions
1
–
2
, if
M=\lceil(n\wedge p)^{1/8}\rceil
, for any
t\in[0,1]
, we have

\mu_{\lfloor nt\rfloor}^{(G)}-\mu_{M,\lfloor nt\rfloor}=o(\omega).

Proof.

Rewrite

\mu_{\lfloor nt\rfloor}^{(G)}=g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{i+h,\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{M}(h)\}.

We have

\displaystyle\left|\mu_{\lfloor nt\rfloor}^{(G)}-\mu_{M,\lfloor nt\rfloor}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\Bigg|\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{i+h,\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{M}(h)\}-\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)\}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(\lfloor nt\rfloor)\Bigg|\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}a_{i,\lfloor nt\rfloor}a_{i+h,\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sum\limits_{\ell=M-h+1}^{\infty}b_{\ell}b_{\ell+h}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})}{n^{2}\sqrt{p}}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|\bigg)\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=M+1}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|\bigg)=o\left\{\sqrt{p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}\right\}

due to the fact
\sum\limits_{\ell=M}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|=o(M^{-4})
with Lemma
8.13
by Assumption
1
–(ii).

L
EMMA
8.5

Under Assumptions
1
–
2
, if
M=\lceil(n\wedge p)^{1/8}\rceil
, for any
t\in[0,1]
, we have

p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n,M}^{2})=p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})\{1+o(1)\}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle{\bf\Omega}_{n}-{\bf\Omega}_{n,M}=\bigg[c_{0}+2\sum\limits_{h=1}^{n}\bigg(1-\frac{h}{n}\bigg)c_{h}-\bigg\{c_{0,M}+2\sum\limits_{h=1}^{M}\bigg(1-\frac{h}{n}\bigg)c_{h,M}\bigg\}\bigg]{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}:=C_{2}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}},

and by Lemma
8.13
, we have

\displaystyle|C_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\bigg|\sum\limits_{h=0}^{n}\bigg(1-\frac{h}{n}\bigg)c_{h}-\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\bigg(1-\frac{h}{n}\bigg)c_{h,M}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\bigg|\sum\limits_{h=M+1}^{n}\bigg(1-\frac{h}{n}\bigg)\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}b_{\ell+h}+\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\bigg(1-\frac{h}{n}\bigg)\sum\limits_{\ell=M-h+1}^{\infty}b_{\ell}b_{\ell+h}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 4\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|\bigg)\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=M+1}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|\bigg)=o(M^{-4}).

By

\displaystyle p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n,M}^{2})-p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})=2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}\left\{{\bf\Omega}_{n,M}({\bf\Omega}_{n,M}-{\bf\Omega}_{n})\right\}-p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}\left\{({\bf\Omega}_{n,M}-{\bf\Omega}_{n})^{2}\right\}.

then,

p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n,M}^{2})=p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})\{1+o(1)\}.

L
EMMA
8.6

Under Assumptions
1
–
2
, if
M=\lceil(n\wedge p)^{1/8}\rceil
, for any
t\in[0,1]
, we have

p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})=p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2})\{1+o(1)\}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle{\bf\Omega}-{\bf\Omega}_{n}=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{n-1}h\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)+\sum_{h=n}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left(n^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{n-1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}hb_{\ell}b_{\ell+h}+\sum_{h=n}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}b_{\ell+h}\right){\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}:=C_{3}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}},

and by Lemma
8.13
, we have

\displaystyle|C_{3}|\leq 2\left|n^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{n-1}h\sum_{\ell=h}^{\infty}b_{\ell}+\sum_{h=n}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=h}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\right|=O(n^{-1})+o(n^{-3})=o(1).

It implies that,

\|{\bf\Omega}_{n}-{\bf\Omega}\|_{F}\leq o(1)\|{\bf\Omega}\|_{F},

where
\|\cdot\|_{F}
denotes the Frobenius norm for a matrix.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\left|\|{\bf\Omega}\|_{F}^{2}-\|{\bf\Omega}_{n}\|_{F}^{2}\right|=\left|(\|{\bf\Omega}\|_{F}-\|{\bf\Omega}_{n}\|_{F})(\|{\bf\Omega}\|_{F}+\|{\bf\Omega}_{n}\|_{F})\right|\leq o(1)\|{\bf\Omega}\|^{2}_{F},

that is,

p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})=p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2})\{1+o(1)\}.

8.3
Proof of Theorem
2

To prove Theorem
2
, it is equivalent to prove Lemmas
8.7
and
8.8
below.

L
EMMA
8.7

Under Assumptions
1
–
2
, if
M=\lceil(n\wedge p)^{1/8}\rceil
,
p=o(n^{7/4})
and
\|{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\|^{2}=o(np^{1/2}/M^{2})
, for any
t\in[0,1]
, we have

\hat{\mu}_{M,\lfloor nt\rfloor}-\mu_{M,\lfloor nt\rfloor}=o_{p}(\omega)
as
(n,p)\rightarrow\infty
.

Proof.

By definition,
\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)\}}
can be decomposed as,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)\}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{t=1}^{n-M-2h-1}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+h}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+2h+1})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{t=\tau-2h-M-1}^{\tau-h}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{t=\tau-M-1}^{\tau+h}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})

\displaystyle+\frac{M}{2n}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}.

Let
k=\lfloor nt\rfloor
, then

\displaystyle\hat{\mu}_{M,k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(k)\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\!\!\sum_{t=1}^{n-M-2h-1}\!\!\!\!(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+h}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+2h+1})\right\}

(17)

\displaystyle-g(k)\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{t=\tau-2h-M-1}^{\tau-h}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})\right\}

(18)

\displaystyle-g(k)\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{t=\tau-M-1}^{\tau+h}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})\right\}

(19)

\displaystyle+g(k)\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\left(\frac{M}{2n}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\right).

(20)

We first show the terms in Equations (
18
)–(
19
) converges to zero with probability 1, and the Equation (
20
) converges to zero in Step 1. Then, we will show that the Equation (
17
) converges to
\mu_{M,k}
with probability 1 in Step 2.

Step 1.

We first consider Equation (
20
), by
\|{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\|^{2}=o(np^{1/2}/M^{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\left|g(k)\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\left(\frac{M}{2n}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\frac{k^{2}(n-k)^{2}}{n^{3}\sqrt{p}}\left(\frac{M}{2n}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\right)\left\{\frac{n(2M+1)}{k(n-k)}-\frac{M(M+1)(n^{2}+k^{2}-nk)}{2k^{2}(n-k)^{2}}\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{M^{2}}{np^{1/2}}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}=o(1).

We next consider Equation (
19
), and Equation (
18
) can be handled in the same way. Notice that,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{t=\tau-M-1}^{\tau+h}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{h=0}^{M}\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}\left\{\frac{n}{k(n-k)}-h\frac{n^{2}+k^{2}-nk}{k^{2}(n-k)^{2}}\right\}\sum_{t=\tau-M-1}^{\tau+h}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1}),

and

\begin{aligned} &\mathbb{E}\left\{{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}+M+h_{1}+1})(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}+M+h_{2}+1})^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\right\}\\
=&{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}\left\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(|t_{1}-t_{2}|)+\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(|t_{1}-t_{2}+h_{1}-h_{2}|)-\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(|t_{1}-t_{2}-h_{2}|)-\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(|t_{1}-t_{2}+h_{1}|)\right\}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\\
\lesssim&{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\end{aligned}.

The expectation of Equation (
19
) is 0, we then calculation the variance.

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left(g(k)\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{t=\tau-M-1}^{\tau+h}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{k^{4}(n-k)^{4}}{4n^{8}p}\sum_{h_{1}=0}^{M}\sum_{h_{2}=0}^{M}\sum_{t_{1}=\tau-M-1}^{\tau+h_{1}}\sum_{t_{2}=\tau-M-1}^{\tau+h_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{k}+\frac{1}{n-k}-h\frac{n^{2}+k^{2}-nk}{k^{2}(n-k)^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\cdot\mathbb{E}\left\{{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}+M+h_{1}+1})(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}+M+h_{2}+1})^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{k^{4}(n-k)^{4}}{4n^{8}p}M^{2}(2M)^{2}\left\{\frac{1}{k}+\frac{1}{n-k}+M\frac{n^{2}+k^{2}-nk}{k^{2}(n-k)^{2}}\right\}^{2}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{M^{4}}{n^{2}p}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}{\boldsymbol{\delta}}=o(1),

that is, the Equation (
19
) converges to 0 with probability 1.

Step 2.

Considering Equation (
17
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{n-M-2h-1}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+h}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+2h+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{n-M-2h-1}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum\limits_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{2}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{t+h_{1}-\ell_{1}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{t-\ell_{2}}-\sum\limits_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{2}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{t+h_{1}-\ell_{1}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{t+M+h_{1}+1-\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{2}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{t+M+2h_{1}+1-\ell_{1}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{t-\ell_{2}}+\sum\limits_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{2}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{t+M+2h_{1}+1-\ell_{1}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{t+M+h_{1}+1-\ell_{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{n-M-2h-1}\Big\{\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{1}+h}\Big)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})-\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{1}+M+2h+1}\Big)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle-\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{1}+M+h+1}\Big)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})+\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{1}+h}\Big)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n-M-2h-1}{2n}\Big\{2\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)\}-\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{1}+M+2h+1}\Big)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})-\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{1}+M+h+1}\Big)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\Big\}.

By Assumption
1
-(ii), we have

\displaystyle\frac{g(k)}{\sqrt{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}}\bigg|\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\frac{n-M-2h-1}{2n}\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{1}+M+2h+1}\Big)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})}{\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}}\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}|b_{\ell_{1}}||b_{\ell_{1}+M+2h+1}|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{p\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}|b_{\ell_{1}}|\Big(\sum\limits_{h=\ell_{1}+M+1}^{\ell_{1}+3M+1}|b_{h}|\Big)\rightarrow 0,

with
\sum\limits_{\ell=M}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|=o(M^{-4})
by Lemma
8.13
, as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
.
Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\frac{g(k)}{\sqrt{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}}\left|\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\frac{n-M-2h-1}{2n}\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell_{1}}b_{\ell_{1}+M+h+1}\Big)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\right|\rightarrow 0.

Thus, as
n,p\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{t=1}^{n-M-2h-1}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+h}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+2h+1})\right\}=\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)\}+o(\omega).

It remains to investigate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n-M-2h-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n-M-2h-1}\mathrm{cov}\bigg[(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+h_{1}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+M+2h_{1}+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+M+h_{1}+1}),

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+h_{2}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+M+2h_{2}+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+M+h_{2}+1})\bigg],

(21)

for any
i,j,h_{1},h_{2}
. Since

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+h_{1}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+M+2h_{1}+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+M+h_{1}+1})(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+h_{2}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+M+2h_{2}+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+M+h_{2}+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\boldsymbol{Z}_{i+h_{1}-\ell}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\boldsymbol{Z}_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-\ell}\Big)^{{\!\top\!}}\Big({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\boldsymbol{Z}_{i-\ell}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\boldsymbol{Z}_{i+M+h_{1}+1-\ell}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\boldsymbol{Z}_{j+h_{2}-\ell}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\boldsymbol{Z}_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-\ell}\Big)^{{\!\top\!}}\Big({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\boldsymbol{Z}_{j-\ell}-{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}b_{\ell}\boldsymbol{Z}_{j+M+h_{2}+1-\ell}\Big),

we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{cov}\left[(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+h_{1}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+M+2h_{1}+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i+M+h_{1}+1}),(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+h_{2}}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+M+2h_{2}+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{j+M+h_{2}+1})\right]

(22)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+h_{1},k_{2}\leq i,k_{3}\leq j+h_{2},k_{4}\leq j}b_{i+h_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i-k_{2}}b_{j+h_{2}-k_{3}}b_{j-k_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+h_{1},k_{2}\leq i,k_{3}\leq j+h_{2},k_{4}\leq j+M+h_{2}+1}b_{i+h_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i-k_{2}}b_{j+h_{2}-k_{3}}b_{j+M+h_{2}+1-k_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+h_{1},k_{2}\leq i,k_{3}\leq j+M+2h_{2}+1,k_{4}\leq j}b_{i+h_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i-k_{2}}b_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3}}b_{j-k_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+h_{1},k_{2}\leq i,k_{3}\leq j+M+2h_{2}+1,k_{4}\leq j+M+h_{2}+1}b_{i+h_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i-k_{2}}b_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3}}b_{j+M+h_{2}+1-k_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+h_{1},k_{2}\leq i+M+h_{1}+1,k_{3}\leq j+h_{2},k_{4}\leq j}b_{i+h_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i+M+h_{1}+1-k_{2}}b_{j+h_{2}-k_{3}}b_{j-k_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+h_{1},k_{2}\leq i+M+h_{1}+1,k_{3}\leq j+h_{2},k_{4}\leq j+M+h_{2}+1}b_{i+h_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i+M+h_{1}+1-k_{2}}b_{j+h_{2}-k_{3}}b_{j+M+h_{2}+1-k_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+h_{1},k_{2}\leq i+M+h_{1}+1,k_{3}\leq j+M+2h_{2}+1,k_{4}\leq j}b_{i+h_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i+M+h_{1}+1-k_{2}}b_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3}}b_{j-k_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+h_{1},k_{2}\leq i+M+h_{1}+1,k_{3}\leq j+M+2h_{2}+1,k_{4}\leq j+M+h_{2}+1}b_{i+h_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i+M+h_{1}+1-k_{2}}b_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3}}b_{j+M+h_{2}+1-k_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+M+2h_{1}+1,k_{2}\leq i,k_{3}\leq j+h_{2},k_{4}\leq j}b_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-k_{1}}b_{i-k_{2}}b_{j+h_{2}-k_{3}}b_{j-k_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+M+2h_{1}+1,k_{2}\leq i,k_{3}\leq j+h_{2},k_{4}\leq j+M+h_{2}+1}b_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-k_{1}}b_{i-k_{2}}b_{j+h_{2}-k_{3}}b_{j+M+h_{2}+1-k_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+M+2h_{1}+1,k_{2}\leq i,k_{3}\leq j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3},k_{4}\leq j}b_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-k_{1}}b_{i-k_{2}}b_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3}}b_{j-k_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+M+2h_{1}+1,k_{2}\leq i,k_{3}\leq j+M+2h_{2}+1,k_{4}\leq j+M+h_{2}+1}b_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-k_{1}}b_{i-k_{2}}b_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3}}b_{j+M+h_{2}+1-k_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+M+2h_{1}+1,k_{2}\leq i+M+h_{1}+1,k_{3}\leq j+h_{2},k_{4}\leq j}b_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-k_{1}}b_{i+M+h_{1}+1-k_{2}}b_{j+h_{2}-k_{3}}b_{j-k_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+M+2h_{1}+1,k_{2}\leq i+M+h_{1}+1,k_{3}\leq j+h_{2},k_{4}\leq j+M+h_{2}+1}b_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-k_{1}}b_{i+M+h_{1}+1-k_{2}}b_{j+h_{2}-k_{3}}b_{j+M+h_{2}+1-k_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+M+2h_{1}+1,k_{2}\leq i+M+h_{1}+1,k_{3}\leq j+M+2h_{2}+1,k_{4}\leq j}b_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-k_{1}}b_{i+M+h_{1}+1-k_{2}}b_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3}}b_{j-k_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i+M+2h_{1}+1,k_{2}\leq i+M+h_{1}+1,k_{3}\leq j+M+2h_{2}+1,k_{4}\leq j+M+h_{2}+1}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!b_{i+M+2h_{1}+1-k_{1}}b_{i+M+h_{1}+1-k_{2}}b_{j+M+2h_{2}+1-k_{3}}b_{j+M+h_{2}+1-k_{4}}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{1}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{2}}-\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2})\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\right\}\left\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{3}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{4}}-\mathbb{I}(k_{3}=k_{4})\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\right\}\right].

Denote
\rho_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}}:=\mathbb{E}\left[\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{1}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{2}}-\mathbb{I}(k_{1}=k_{2})\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\}\{\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{3}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{4}}-\mathbb{I}(k_{3}=k_{4})\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\}\right]
, we have

\displaystyle\rho_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}}\begin{cases}=\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2}),&k_{1}=k_{3}=r,\,k_{2}=k_{4}=s,\,r\neq s,\\
=\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2}),,&k_{1}=k_{4}=r,\,k_{2}=k_{3}=s,\,r\neq s,\\
\leq\tau_{1}\mathrm{tr}^{2}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}),&k_{1}=k_{2}=k_{3}=k_{4}=r,\\
=0,&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

We only need to calculate the coefficients of
\rho_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}}
in (
8.3
), denoted as
C_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}}
. We will consider the first term in (
22
), the analysis of the others are similar.

\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-M-2h_{1}-1}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n-M-2h_{2}-1}|C_{r,s,r,s}|\leq\!\!\!\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-M-2h_{1}-1}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n-M-2h_{2}-1}\!\!\!\!\sum\limits_{r\leq(i+h_{1})\wedge(j+h_{2}),s\leq i\wedge j}\!\!\!\!|b_{i+h_{1}-r}b_{i-s}b_{j+h_{2}-r}b_{j-s}|

We rewrite
k_{1}=i+h_{1}-r,k_{2}=i-s,k_{3}=j+h_{2}-r,k_{4}=j-s
. Fix
(i,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4})
,
(r,s,j)
will be uniquely determined or not exist.
Thus,

\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-M-2h_{1}-1}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n-M-2h_{2}-1}|C_{r,s,r,s}|\leq n\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|\Big)^{4}.

Similarly,

\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-M-2h_{1}-1}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n-M-2h_{2}-1}|C_{r,s,s,r}|\leq n\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|\Big)^{4},\quad\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-M-2h_{1}-1}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n-M-2h_{2}-1}|C_{r,r,r,r}|\leq\Big(\sum\limits_{\ell=0}^{\infty}|b_{\ell}|\Big)^{4}.

By
\mathrm{tr}^{2}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})\lesssim p\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})
and
p=o(n^{7/4})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left(g(k)\sum\limits_{h=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n-h}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h=0)\}}a_{i,k}a_{i+h,k}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\!\!\sum_{t=1}^{n-M-2h-1}\!\!\!\!(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+h+1})^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+h}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t+M+2h+1})\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{2}(k)\sum\limits_{h_{1}=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{h_{2}=0}^{M}\sum\limits_{i_{1}=1}^{n-h_{1}}\sum\limits_{i_{2}=1}^{n-h_{2}}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h_{1}=0)\}}{\{2-\mathbb{I}(h_{2}=0)\}}a_{i_{1},k}a_{i_{1}+h_{1},k}a_{i_{2},k}a_{i_{2}+h_{2},k}\times\eqref{L8-6-0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\bigg\{\frac{M^{2}(n+p)}{n^{2}p}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})\bigg\}=o(\omega^{2}).

L
EMMA
8.8

Under Assumptions
1
–
2
and
H_{0}
, if
M=\lceil(n\wedge p)^{1/8}\rceil
and
p=o(n^{7/4})
, for any
t\in[0,1]
, we have

\hat{\omega}/\omega\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\rightarrow}}1
as
(n,p)\rightarrow\infty
.

Proof.

Recall

\displaystyle\hat{\omega}=\bigg\{\frac{2}{p}\bigg(\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(0)\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(0)\}}+2\sum\limits_{h=1}^{M}\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(0)\}}+2\sum\limits_{k=1}^{M}\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(0)\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(k)\}}+4\sum\limits_{h,k=1}^{M}\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(k)\}}\bigg)\bigg\}^{1/2},

where

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h)\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(k)\}}=\frac{\sum\limits_{t=1}^{[n/2]-M-2k-1}\sum\limits_{s=t+[n/2]}^{n-M-2k-1}\breve{\boldsymbol{X}}_{t,h,s,k}\breve{\boldsymbol{X}}_{t+h,h,s+k,k}}{4(n-k-3/2[n/2]-M/2)([n/2]-M-2k-1)},

with
\breve{\boldsymbol{X}}_{f,h,g,k}=(\boldsymbol{X}_{f}-\boldsymbol{X}_{f+M+h+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{g}-\boldsymbol{X}_{g+M+k+1})
. Define
\breve{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{f,h,g,k}=(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{f}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{f+M+h+1})^{{\!\top\!}}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{g}-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{g+M+k+1})
.
We decompose
\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h_{1})\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h_{2})\}}=\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2}}+R_{h_{1},h_{2}}
,
where

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2}}=\sum\limits_{i_{1}=1}^{[n/2]-M-2h_{2}-1}\sum\limits_{i_{2}=h_{1}+[n/2]}^{n-M-2h_{2}-1}\frac{\breve{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{i_{1},h_{1},i_{2},h_{2}}\breve{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{i_{1}+h_{1},h_{1},i_{2}+h_{2},h_{2}}}{4(n-h_{2}-3/2[n/2]-M/2)([n/2]-M-2h_{2}-1)},

and
R_{h_{1},h_{2}}:=\widehat{\mathrm{tr}\{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h_{1})\boldsymbol{\Gamma}(h_{2})\}}-\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2}}
. Following the similar analysis in Lemma
8.7
, it is naturally to prove that
R_{h_{1},h_{2}}=o_{p}(1)
, and the details are omitted. Now, we turn to investigate
\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2}}:=\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},H}+\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},L}
, where
\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},H}
and
\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},L}
represent the terms constructed by the high order and low order of
Z_{ij}
.

\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2}}
is the sum of 16 terms. Here, we only consider the first term here, the others can be analyzed similarly.
The first term in
\breve{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{i_{1},h_{1},i_{2},h_{2}}\breve{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{i_{1}+h_{1},h_{1},i_{2}+h_{2},h_{2}}
is

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{k_{1}\leq i_{1},k_{2}\leq i_{2},k_{3}\leq i_{1}+h_{1},k_{4}\leq i_{2}+h_{2}}b_{i_{1}-k_{1}}b_{i_{2}-k_{2}}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-k_{3}}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-k_{4}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{1}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{2}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{3}}^{{\!\top\!}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\boldsymbol{Z}_{k_{4}}.

(23)

The high order term of Equation (
23
) is

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p}\sigma_{j_{1}j_{1}}\sigma_{j_{2}j_{2}}\bigg\{\bigg(\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge i_{2}\wedge(i_{1}+h_{1})\\
k\leq i_{2}+h_{2}\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-r}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-k}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge i_{2}\wedge(i_{2}+h_{2})\\
k\leq i_{1}+h_{1}\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-r}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-k}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-r}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge(i_{1}+h_{1})\wedge(i_{2}+h_{2})\\
k\leq i_{2}\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-k}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-r}\bigg)Z_{rj_{1}}^{3}Z_{kj_{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge i_{2}\wedge(i_{1}+h_{1})\wedge(i_{2}+h_{2})\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-r}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-r}Z_{rj_{1}}^{4}\bigg\},

where
\sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}
is
(j_{1},j_{2})
th components of
{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}
.
By Assumption
1
-(i), we have
\mathbb{E}|\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},H}|=O(p^{2}n^{-2})
.
Furthermore,
we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\bigg|\tilde{S}_{0,0,H}+2\sum\limits_{h_{1}=1}^{M}\tilde{S}_{h_{1},0,H}+2\sum\limits_{h_{2}=1}^{M}\tilde{S}_{0,h_{2},H}+4\sum\limits_{h_{1}=1}^{M}\sum\limits_{h_{2}=1}^{M}\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},H}\bigg|}{\bigg(c_{0}^{2}+4\sum\limits_{r=1}^{M}c_{0}c_{r}+4\sum\limits_{r=1}^{M}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{M}c_{r}c_{s}\bigg)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})}=O(pn^{-2})=o(1).

Besides that, the expectation of the low order term of Equation (
23
) is

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p}\sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge(i_{1}+h_{1})\\
k\leq i_{2}\wedge(i_{2}+h_{2})\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-k}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-k}+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge(i_{2}+h_{2})\\
k\leq i_{2}\wedge(i_{1}+h_{1})\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-k}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-k}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-r}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p}\sigma_{j_{1}j_{1}}\sigma_{j_{2}j_{2}}\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge i_{2}\\
k\leq(i_{1}+h_{1})\wedge(i_{2}+h_{2})\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-r}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-k}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-k}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum\limits_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}=1}^{p}\sigma_{j_{1}j_{1}}\sigma_{j_{2}j_{2}}+2\sum\limits_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}=1}^{p}\sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Big)\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge i_{2}\wedge(i_{1}+h_{1})\wedge(i_{2}+h_{2})\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-r}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-r}\Big\}.

By Assumption
1
-(ii) again,

\displaystyle 4(n-h_{2}-3/2[n/2]-M/2)([n/2]-M-2h_{2}-1)\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},L}-a_{h_{1}}a_{h_{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i_{1}=1}^{[n/2]-M-2h_{2}-1}\sum\limits_{i_{2}=t+[n/2]}^{n-M-2h_{2}-1}\bigg\{c_{i_{2}+h_{2}-i_{1}}c_{i_{1}+h_{1}-i_{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})+c_{i_{1}-i_{2}}c_{i_{2}-i_{1}+h_{2}-h_{1}}\mathrm{tr}^{2}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle+\bigg(\sum\limits_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}=1}^{p}\sigma_{j_{1}j_{1}}\sigma_{j_{2}j_{2}}+2\sum\limits_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}=1}^{p}\sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\bigg)\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\leq i_{1}\wedge i_{2}\wedge(i_{1}+h_{1})\wedge(i_{2}+h_{2})\end{subarray}}b_{i_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}-r}b_{i_{1}+h_{1}-r}b_{i_{2}+h_{2}-r}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(p^{2}n^{-1}).

Hence,

\mathbb{E}\{(\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},L}-c_{h_{1}}c_{h_{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2}))/c_{h_{1}}c_{h_{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})\}=o(pn^{-3})=o(1)
. Similarly, we can prove that

\mathrm{var}\{(\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},L}-c_{h_{1}}c_{h_{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2}))/c_{h_{1}}c_{h_{2}}\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})\}=o(pn^{-2}+n^{-1})=o(1)
. Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{S}_{0,0,L}+2\sum\limits_{h_{1}=1}^{M}\tilde{S}_{h_{1},0,L}+2\sum\limits_{h_{2}=1}^{M}\tilde{S}_{0,h_{2},L}+4\sum\limits_{h_{1}=1}^{M}\sum\limits_{h_{2}=1}^{M}\tilde{S}_{h_{1},h_{2},L}}{\bigg(c_{0}^{2}+4\sum\limits_{r=1}^{M}c_{0}c_{r}+4\sum\limits_{r=1}^{M}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{M}c_{r}c_{s}\bigg)\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})}=1+o_{p}(1).

Above all, we get
\hat{\omega}/\omega\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\rightarrow}}1
.

8.4
Proof of Theorem
3

Proof.

By the continuous mapping theorem and Theorem
1
–
2
, since
h(x)=\mathrm{sup}_{t}x(t)
is continuous in the space
D[0,1]
when
x(t)\in D[0,1]
, we have

\displaystyle S_{n,p}/\hat{\omega}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\rightarrow}}\max_{t\in[0,1]}V(t).

8.5
Proof of Proposition
2.1

By Theorem
2
–
3
, we have

\displaystyle S_{n,p}/\hat{\omega}=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq k\leq n}\left\{W(k)-\hat{\mu}_{M,k}\right\}/\hat{\omega}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left\{W(\tau)-\hat{\mu}_{M,\tau}\right\}/\hat{\omega}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\tau^{2}(n-\tau)^{2}}{n^{3}\sqrt{p}}(\|{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\|-\|\frac{1}{\tau}\sum_{i=1}^{\tau}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i}-\frac{1}{n-\tau}\sum_{i=\tau+1}^{n}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i}\|)^{2}/\hat{\omega}-\hat{\mu}_{M,\tau}/\hat{\omega}

\displaystyle\asymp

\displaystyle p^{-1/2}n\|{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\|^{2}+O_{p}(1).

Thus, the test based on
S_{n,p}
is consistent provided that
\|{\boldsymbol{\delta}}\|^{2}\gtrsim p^{1/2}n^{-1}
.

8.6
Proof of Theorem
5

Proof.

Let
t=k/n
, recall

\displaystyle\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,k/n}=\frac{1}{w_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}_{\ell,k/n},

with
{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}_{i,k/n}=a_{i,k}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i}
. According to the proof of Theorem 1, we have

\displaystyle\omega^{-1}\{W(k)-\mu_{M,k}\}=\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{1,k/n},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},k/n})+o_{p}(1),

where
\{\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{i,k/n}\}_{i=1}^{n}
are independent and

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{1,k/n},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},k/n})=\frac{2g(k)}{\sqrt{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}}\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}_{\ell,k}\bigg)^{\top}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}_{\ell,k}\bigg).

Define
Z^{(NG)}_{0,j}=\max\limits_{k=1,\dots,n-1}|C^{(NG)}_{0,j}(k)|
by substituting
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{i}
for
\boldsymbol{X}_{i}
in
|C_{0,j}(k)|
, it suffices to show that
\max\limits_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{1,k/n},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\zeta}}}_{k_{n},k/n})
and
\max\limits_{j=1,\ldots,p}Z^{(NG)}_{0,j}
are asymptotically independent.

8.6.1
Gaussian sequence.

For any fixed
x,y\in\mathbb{R}
, we define

\displaystyle A_{p}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle A_{p}(x)=\Big\{\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,k/n},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},k/n})\leq x\Big\},

\displaystyle B_{j}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle B_{j}(y)=\Big\{Z^{(G)}_{0,j}>\mu_{p}\{\exp(-y)\}\Big\}.

As
\mathrm{P}(\cup_{j=1}^{p}B_{j})\rightarrow 1-\exp\{\exp(-y)\}
and
\mathrm{P}(A_{p})\rightarrow F_{V}(x)
, we only need to show that

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\bigg(\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,k/n},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},k/n})\leq x,\max_{j=1,\ldots,p}\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}|C^{(G)}_{0,j}(k)|\leq\mu_{p}\{\exp(-y)\}\bigg)

\displaystyle\rightarrow F_{V}(x)\cdot\exp\{\exp(-y)\},

or, equivalently,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}A_{p}B_{j}\bigg)\rightarrow F_{V}(x)\cdot[1-\exp\{\exp(-y)\}].

For each
d\geq 1
, we define

\displaystyle\zeta_{AB}(p,d)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<\dots<j_{d}\leq p}\left|\mathrm{P}(A_{p}B_{j_{1}}\dots B_{j_{d}})-\mathrm{P}(A_{p})\cdot\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\dots B_{j_{d}})\right|,

\displaystyle H(p,d)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<\dots<j_{d}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\dots B_{j_{d}}).

By the inclusion-exclusion principle, we observe that, for any integer
k\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}A_{p}B_{j}\bigg)\leq\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(A_{p}B_{j_{1}})-\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<j_{2}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(A_{p}B_{j_{1}}B_{j_{2}})+\dots

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<\dots<j_{2k+1}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(A_{p}B_{j_{1}}\dots B_{j_{2k+1}}),

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}B_{j}\bigg)\geq\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}})-\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<j_{2}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}B_{j_{2}})+\dots

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<\dots<j_{2k}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\dots B_{j_{2k}}).

Then,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}A_{p}B_{j}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p})\bigg\{\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}})-\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<j_{2}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}B_{j_{2}})+\dots

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<\dots<j_{2k}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\dots B_{j_{2k}})\bigg\}+\sum\limits_{d=1}^{2k}\zeta_{AB}(p,d)+H(p,2k+1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p})\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}B_{j}\bigg)+\sum\limits_{d=1}^{2k}\zeta_{AB}(p,d)+H(p,2k+1).

By using similar arguments, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}A_{p}B_{j}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p})\bigg\{\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}})-\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<j_{2}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}B_{j_{2}})+\dots

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{1\leq j_{1}<\dots<j_{2k}\leq p}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\dots B_{j_{2k}})\bigg\}+\sum\limits_{d=1}^{2k}\zeta_{AB}(p,d)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p})\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}B_{j}\bigg)+\sum\limits_{d=1}^{2k}\zeta_{AB}(p,d).

According to Equations (S.5) and (S.6) in
Wang and Feng 2023
and the following Lemma
8.9
, by fixing
k
and letting
p\rightarrow\infty
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathop{\lim\mathrm{sup}}\limits_{p\rightarrow\infty}\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}A_{p}B_{j}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle F_{W}(x)\cdot[1-\exp\{\exp(-y)\}],

\displaystyle\mathop{\lim\mathrm{inf}}\limits_{p\rightarrow\infty}\mathrm{P}\bigg(\mathop{\bigcup}\limits_{j=1}^{p}A_{p}B_{j}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle F_{W}(x)\cdot[1-\exp\{\exp(-y)\}].

L
EMMA
8.9

Under the conditions in Theorem 5, then for each
d\geq 1
, as
p\rightarrow\infty
, we have
\zeta_{AB}(p,d)\rightarrow 0
.

Proof.

For
i=1,\dots,n
, we define
{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(1)}=({\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,j_{1}},\dots,{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,j_{d}})^{\top}
and
{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(2)}=({\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,j_{d+1}},\dots,{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,j_{p}})^{\top}
. For
k,l\in\{1,2\}
, define
\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{kl}(0):=\mathrm{cov}({\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(k)},{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(l)})=c_{h}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{kl}
. Since
\boldsymbol{U}_{i}:=(U_{i,1},\dots,U_{i,p-d})^{\top}={\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(2)}-\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{21}(0)\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{11}(0)^{-1}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(1)}
and
\boldsymbol{V}_{i}=(V_{i,1},\dots,V_{i,p-d})^{\top}:=\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{21}(0)\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{11}(0)^{-1}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(1)}
are independent, we partition
{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(2)}
into
{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(2)}=\boldsymbol{U}_{i}+\boldsymbol{V}_{i}
. Recall that

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}(\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{1,k/n},\dots,\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{k_{n},k/n})=

\displaystyle 2\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}g(k)(w_{n}-M)^{2}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,k/n}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,k/n}/\sqrt{2p^{-1}\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})}.

Then,

\displaystyle\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)(w_{n}-M)^{2}\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{i,k/n}^{\top}\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\eta}}}_{j,k/n}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}a_{\ell,k}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell}\bigg)^{\top}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell,k}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell}\bigg)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}\!\!\!\!a_{\ell_{1},k}a_{\ell_{2},k}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}\!\!\!\!a_{\ell_{1},k}a_{\ell_{2},k}(\boldsymbol{U}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{U}_{\ell_{2}}+{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{1},(1)}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{2},(1)}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~+2\boldsymbol{U}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{V}_{\ell_{2}}+\boldsymbol{V}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{V}_{\ell_{2}})\bigg\}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle~S_{n,p,1}+\Theta,

where
S_{n,p,1}:=\max\limits_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell_{1},k}a_{\ell_{2},k}\boldsymbol{U}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{U}_{\ell_{2}}\bigg\}
and

\displaystyle\Theta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell_{1},k}a_{\ell_{2},k}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{1},(1)}^{\top}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell_{2},(1)}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell_{1},k}a_{\ell_{2},k}\boldsymbol{V}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{V}_{\ell_{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+2\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\sum\limits_{\ell_{1}=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}\sum\limits_{\ell_{2}=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell_{1},k}a_{\ell_{2},k}\boldsymbol{U}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{V}_{\ell_{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\Theta_{1}+\Theta_{2}+\Theta_{3}.

We claim that, for any
\epsilon>0
and sufficiently large
p
, there exists a sequence of positive constants
c:=c_{p}>0
with
c_{p}\rightarrow\infty
such that

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(|\Theta_{i}|\geq\epsilon\omega)\leq p^{-c},\quad i=1,2,3,

(24)

which will be shown in later. Consequently,
\mathrm{P}(|\Theta|\geq\epsilon\omega)\leq p^{-c}
for some
c\rightarrow\infty
and sufficiently large
p
.

By claim (
24
), due to the independence of random vectors
\boldsymbol{U}_{i}
and
{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{i,(1)}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x)B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x)B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}},|\Theta|<\epsilon\omega)+p^{-c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(S_{n,p,1}/\omega\leq x+\epsilon,B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})+p^{-c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(S_{n,p,1}/\omega\leq x+\epsilon)\cdot\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})+p^{-c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x+2\epsilon))\cdot\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})+2p^{-c}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(S_{n,p,1}/\omega\leq x-\epsilon)\cdot\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(S_{n,p,1}/\omega\leq x-\epsilon,B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(S_{n,p,1}/\omega\leq x-\epsilon,B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}},|\Theta|<\epsilon\omega)+p^{-c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x)B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})+p^{-c}.

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x-2\epsilon))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x-2\epsilon),|\Theta|<\epsilon\omega)+p^{-c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(S_{n,p,1}/\omega\leq x-\epsilon)+p^{-c}.

Then,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x)B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x-2\epsilon))\cdot\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})-2p^{-c}.

Since
\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x))
is increasing in
x
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x)B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})-\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x))\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x+2\epsilon))-\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x-2\epsilon))\right\}\mathrm{P}(B_{j_{1}}\cdots B_{j_{d}})+2p^{-c}.

Since for any
d\geq 1
,
H(p,d)\rightarrow(d!)^{-1}\exp(-dx/2)
as
p\rightarrow\infty
and
\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x+2\epsilon))-\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x-2\epsilon))\rightarrow 0
as
p\rightarrow\infty
and
\epsilon\rightarrow 0
, we have

\displaystyle\zeta_{AB}(p,d)\leq\{\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x+2\epsilon))-\mathrm{P}(A_{p}(x-2\epsilon))\}\cdot H(p,d)+2\binom{p}{d}p^{-c}\rightarrow 0,

as
p\rightarrow\infty
for any
d\geq 1
.

Proof of Equation (
24
).

Let

\displaystyle W_{1,(1)}(k)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{k^{2}(n-k)^{2}}{n^{3}\sqrt{p}}\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}a_{\ell,k}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell,(1)}\bigg)^{\top}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell,k}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell,(1)}\bigg).

Define
{\bf\Omega}_{w_{n}-M,M,1}:=\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{11}(0)+2\sum\nolimits_{h=1}^{n}(1-\frac{|h|}{n})\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{11}(h)
. Similar to the proof of Proposition
8.2
, we define

\displaystyle\varsigma(k)^{2}:=\frac{k^{2}(n-k)^{2}}{n^{4}}\cdot\frac{\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M,1})}{p},

then we have
\mathrm{var}\{W_{1,(1)}(k)\}=\varsigma(k)^{2}\{1+o(1)\}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}(|\Theta_{1}|\geq\epsilon\omega)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\bigg(\bigg|\max_{k=1,\dots,n-1}\bigg\{g(k)\sum\limits_{1\leq i<j\leq k_{n}}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=(i-1)w_{n}+1}^{iw_{n}-M}a_{\ell,k}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell,(1)}\bigg)^{\top}\bigg(\sum\limits_{\ell=(j-1)w_{n}+1}^{jw_{n}-M}a_{\ell,k}{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\ell,(1)}\bigg)\bigg\}\bigg|\geq\epsilon\sqrt{2\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})/p}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\mathrm{P}\bigg(\bigg|\frac{n^{2}}{k(n-k)}\cdot\frac{W_{1,(1)}(k)}{\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M,1})/p}}\bigg|\geq\epsilon\frac{n^{2}}{k(n-k)}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{2\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}_{n}^{2})/p}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}({\bf\Omega}^{2}_{w_{n}-M,M,1})/p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathrm{P}\bigg(\bigg|\frac{W_{1,(1)}(k)}{\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2}_{11})/p}}\bigg|\geq C_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})}{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{11}^{2})}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\exp\bigg\{-C_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})}{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{11}^{2})}}\bigg\}\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg(\exp\bigg|\frac{W_{1,(1)}(k)}{\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2}_{11})/p}}\bigg|\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\exp\bigg\{-C_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2})}{\mathrm{tr}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{11}^{2})}}\bigg\}\cdot\mathrm{log}n\rightarrow 0,

with Assumption
1
and condition
\mathrm{log}n=o(p^{1/4})
, where the last inequality follows with the fact

\displaystyle p^{1/2}\mathrm{tr}^{-1/2}({\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{2}_{11})W_{1,(1)}(k)/\mathrm{log}\mathrm{log}n\rightarrow 0,\quad a.s.

by the law of the iterated logarithm of zero-mean square integrable martingale (see Theorem 4.8 in
Hall and Heyde 2014
).
Similarly, by
\mathrm{log}n=o(p^{1/4})
, we have
